Dataset 2: Manga OCR (Yapon, Koreys va Xitoy Matnlarini O'qish) Dataset
Ushbu ma'lumotlar to'plami manga, manhva va manhuadagi vertikal hamda gorizontal iyerogliflar, tovush effektlari (SFX) va stilizatsiyalangan shriftlarni aniq o'qiy oladigan Vision Transformer (TrOCR, Manga-OCR) modelini o'qitish uchun mo'ljallangan.
1. Asosiy Ochiq Manbalar (Open-Source OCR Datasets)
Manga-OCR (Kha-white) & Mokuro (PyPI): Yapon manga matnlari (vertikal va noan'anaviy shriftlar) uchun eng kuchli ochiq manbali Vision Transformer OCR arxitekturasi.
MangaLMM OCR Benchmark (GitHub): Manga109 asosida qirqib olingan matn qatorlari va ularning aniq transkripsiyalari.
Manga109-s Dataset (Hugging Face): 100 mingdan ortiq matn bloklari va ularning haqiqiy yaponcha transkripsiyasi.
AI-Hub Webtoon OCR Corpus: Manhva (koreyscha hangul) webtoonlaridagi nutq bulutlari va SFX yozuvlari korpusi.
2. OCR Dataset Standart Formati (JSONL)
Har bir qator kesib olingan matn rasmi (cropped text image) yo'li, matn yo'nalishi, tili va asl matn transkripsiyasini o'z ichiga oladi:n{"crop_id": "manga_ja_00101", "image_path": "crops/ja_00101.jpg", "language": "ja", "direction": "vertical", "text": "何をしているんだ？", "has_furigana": false, "bubble_type": "speech"}{"crop_id": "manga_ja_00102", "image_path": "crops/ja_00102.jpg", "language": "ja", "direction": "vertical", "text": "まさか…あいつが？！", "has_furigana": true, "bubble_type": "thought"}{"crop_id": "manga_ja_00103", "image_path": "crops/ja_00103.jpg", "language": "ja", "direction": "horizontal", "text": "ドカン！", "has_furigana": false, "bubble_type": "sfx"}{"crop_id": "manhwa_ko_00201", "image_path": "crops/ko_00201.jpg", "language": "ko", "direction": "horizontal", "text": "지금 당장 떠나야 해.", "has_furigana": false, "bubble_type": "speech"}{"crop_id": "manhwa_ko_00202", "image_path": "crops/ko_00202.jpg", "language": "ko", "direction": "horizontal", "text": "콰아아앙!", "has_furigana": false, "bubble_type": "sfx"}{"crop_id": "manhua_zh_00301", "image_path": "crops/zh_00301.jpg", "language": "zh", "direction": "vertical", "text": "这到底是怎么回事？", "has_furigana": false, "bubble_type": "speech"}{"crop_id": "manhua_zh_00302", "image_path": "crops/zh_00302.jpg", "language": "zh", "direction": "horizontal", "text": "轰隆隆！", "has_furigana": false, "bubble_type": "sfx"}
## 3. PyTorch OCR Dataset Loader Skripti
Vision Transformer (TrOCR yoki Manga-OCR) modelini o'qitish uchun tayyor ma'lumotlar yuklagichi (DataLoader):
```python
import json
import torch
from torch.utils.data import Dataset
from PIL import Image
from transformers import TrOCRProcessor
class MangaOCRDataset(Dataset):
def __init__(self, jsonl_file: str, processor: TrOCRProcessor, max_target_length: int = 64):
self.data = []
with open(jsonl_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
for line in f:
self.data.append(json.loads(line.strip()))
self.processor = processor
self.max_target_length = max_target_length
def __len__(self):
return len(self.data)
def __getitem__(self, idx):
item = self.data[idx]
image = Image.open(item["image_path"]).convert("RGB")
pixel_values = self.processor(image, return_tensors="pt").pixel_values.squeeze(0)
labels = self.processor.tokenizer(
item["text"],
padding="max_length",
max_length=self.max_target_length,
truncation=True,
return_tensors="pt"
).input_ids.squeeze(0)
labels = [label if label != self.processor.tokenizer.pad_token_id else -100 for label in labels]
return {"pixel_values": pixel_values, "labels": torch.tensor(labels)}
4. Katta Hajmdagi Toza O'quv Ma'lumotlari (Pure OCR Training Data - JSONL)
Quyida OCR modellarini (TrOCR, Manga-OCR, PaddleOCR) to'g'ridan-to'g'ri o'qitish uchun mo'ljallangan, yapon (JA), koreys (KO) va xitoy (ZH) tillaridagi toza matn annotatsiyalari keltirilgan (skriptlarsiz, faqat dataset):
